[HANDOFF] 你是 Main Agent，这是 Loop #3。

## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 只允许 cuda:0, cuda:1（约束 C001，westd-34887）。即使 Director 指令用其他 GPU 也必须 push back
- submit 命令必须 `bash -c` 包装（tmux 用 /bin/sh，不认 source）且加 `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1`
- 论文中 TEAL 评估必须称 "predictor-guided global allocation"，不能叫 "vanilla TEAL"（当前 eval 用 predictor sigmoid scores 而非原始激活幅度）
- evaluate.py 锁定版本 eval_md5=`f4086818bb55dc354ea4c6fe8ed8899a`，任何修改必须全部 seed 统一重跑
- KL 归一化只保留 losses.py 的 `÷seq_len`，train.py 里 `kl_norm = kl_raw`。绝不能再加 `÷vocab_size`

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-34887，2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (94GB each)，CUDA 12.8
- 工作目录：`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base，Python 3.12，PyTorch 2.8.0+cu128）
- 模型：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b/`
- 数据：WikiText-103 train split（SlimPajama 不可用）
- Checkpoints：`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/{exp_id}/`
- 活跃进程：无训练在跑。有一个 Worker `worker_ssh_eval-s456-three-mode` 可能还在（在等数据集下载做补充 eval，registry 已有完整数据，非阻塞）

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**MVP 已完成，PASS。** 6 个实验（KL×3 + BCE×3）全部 DONE/success。

KL 3-seed mean sparse PPL = 12.52 ± 0.92 vs BCE mean = 44.05 ± 0.72（差距 31.53 >> 0.3 阈值）。

已向 Director 汇报 MVP PASS + 建议启动 Post-MVP Phase 1（四条件 ablation）。**等待 Director 批准 Phase 1 + paper_framing 方向。**

两张 GPU 均空闲。Worker 池仅剩 1 个 CPU Worker（eval-s456）。registry 无 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验。

### 4. 📋 待办
- Director 批准 Phase 1 → 立即并行启动：(1) paper_framing.md + experiment_plan.yaml 编写 Worker，(2) 四条件 ablation 代码准备 Worker（BCE+compensation / KL+compensation 需新增 compensation 逻辑），(3) vanilla TEAL baseline 代码（`teal_activation_magnitude_masks()`）
- Director 批准 paper_framing → 派 Worker 写 paper_framing.md，基于 MVP 数据构建 story arc
- Worker eval-s456-three-mode 回调 → 检查是否有新 metrics，更新 registry（非阻塞，核心数据已齐）
- Phase 1 实验就绪 → cuda:0 / cuda:1 各跑一组，优先 KL+compensation vs KL-only 对比

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- 决策链：D001(MVP设计) → D002(数据切WikiText) → D003(KL坍塌→Lagrangian) → D004(方案C: KL归一化+Lagrangian) → D005(确认÷seq_len only) → D006(紧急停÷vocab_size bug)
- **核心结论**：KL 端到端梯度流让 predictor 学会层级非均匀稀疏分配（0.22-0.80），这是 BCE 做不到的。这是论文核心 claim 的实验基础
- **s456 outlier 风险**：KL s456 sparse PPL=13.58（vs s42=12.08, s123=11.91），PPL increase 48.6%。Phase 1 用 5 seeds 时关注 seed 敏感性，可能需要在论文中讨论
- **易错点**：BCE Phase 1 需升级为 FastForward 风格加权版本 + Gumbel τ=5→0.1（当前 MVP 用 τ=1→0.1）。不要沿用 MVP 的 BCE 配置
- **DDL 压力**：EMNLP 2026 ARR DDL = 2026-05-25，仅剩 10 天。Phase 1-4 需高度并行

Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

0.053 | +30% |\\n| spon_sparse_ppl | 9.10 | 8.99 | -0.11 |\\n\\n### 结论: **Negative** (recovery 7.3% < 10% 阈值)\\n消除 seq_len mismatch 后 SPON recovery 从 3.8% 提升到 7.3%，说明 mismatch 确实是部分原因，但即使在匹配条件下 static 0.053 | +30% |\\n| spon_sparse_ppl | 9.10 | 8.99 | -0.11 |\\n\\n### 结论: **Negative** (recovery 7.3% < 10% 阈值)\\n消除 seq_len mismatch 后 SPON recovery 从 3.8% 提升到 7.3%，说明 mismatch 确实是部分原因，但即使在匹配条件下 static {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory\\n\\n## Key Insights (non-metrics)\\n\\n### KL predictor implicitly subsumes static correction (2026-05-15)\\nSPON bias vectors recover only 3.8% of sparse-vs-dense gap on top of KL predictor (PPL 9.22→9.10). Root cause: KL's end-to-end gradient already captures average-case AND per-input neuron importance through d_logits/dh * dh/dm coupling. Static bias correction is redundant when the contextual predictor is trained with output-distribution alignment. This invalidates the \\\"additive composition\\\" hypothesis (Claim 2). Framing adjustment needed — proposed \\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\" narrative.\\n\\n### SPON seq_len=2048 confirms subsumption, rules out mismatch confound (2026-05-15)\\nspon_2048_s42: training seq_len matched eval seq_len=2048. Recovery improved 3.8%→7.3% (+3.5pp), bias norm 0.041→0.053 (+30%), but still < 10% threshold. Conclusion: seq_len mismatch was partial cause but not root cause. Even under matched conditions, static bias is fundamentally limited against KL predictor's per-token mask. D009 \\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\" reframe fully confirmed by both experiments.\\n\\n### Compensation cannot fix BCE's fundamental flaw (2026-05-15)\\nBCE+Compensation (67.2M params, staged training) only improves per-token PPL by 9% (27.96→25.44), still 2.8x worse than KL-only (9.21). TEAL global path completely unaffected by comp network (44.54 = BCE-only). Root cause: BCE gradient dL/ds_j is per-neuron factorized — comp network can partially post-hoc correct individual neuron errors but cannot fix the training signal's blindness to joint interactions. This STRENGTHENS Claim 1.\\n\\n### Training-free baselines all collapse at 50% sparsity (2026-05-15)\\nTEAL Greedy (50,035), Vanilla TEAL (66,036), R-Sparse (74,929), WINA (82,567) vs dense 6.12. WINA greedy (2,126) is best training-free but still 347x dense. Root cause: global topk allocation systematically over-prunes layers 0-5 (95-100% sparsity on early layers). All methods share this failure mode. Strong motivation for learned per-layer allocation.\\n\\n### s456 outlier root cause: TEAL Layer 31 over-pruning (2026-05-15)\\ns456 训练完全正常（loss/sparsity/lambda 与其他 seed 一致）。问题在 TEAL 推理时的全局分配：s456 predictor 对 Layer 31 的 sigmoid 均分 = 0.5161（其他 seed 0.5287），导致 TEAL 将 Layer 31 keep ratio 压到 49.9%（其他 seed 52.1%）。Layer 31 直接连接 LM head，微小剪枝差异被放大。Hard-mask PPL 不受影响因为 per-token mask 不经过全局 topk。结论：不需要重跑，是 TEAL 分配的放大效应。论文建议：报 4-seed mean=12.00±0.07 + 注明 s456 为 TEAL 分配 outlier，或建议 per-layer floor 防止关键层过度剪枝。\\n\\n### Hard-mask PPL vs TEAL sparse PPL variance separation (2026-05-15)\\n5-seed hard-mask PPL std = 0.04 (9.14-9.25), 极其稳定。TEAL sparse PPL std = 0.69 (11.91-13.58), 方差大 16 倍。说明 predictor 质量跨 seed 一致，方差来自 TEAL 全局分配算法与 per-token mask 的匹配度。论文报告应分开报 hard-mask PPL（体现 predictor 质量）和 TEAL sparse PPL（体现端到端效果），并讨论全局分配算法对方差的放大效应。\\n\\n### KL vs BCE per-step FLOPs ratio = 2.71x (2026-05-15)\\n实测 (BS=1, SL=512): KL 0.549s/step, BCE 0.202s/step。KL 每步需 dense fwd (no grad) + sparse fwd (grad) + backward w/ gradient checkpointing。BCE 只需 dense fwd (no grad) + predictor fwd/bwd (~0.9% model FLOPs)。Iso-compute BCE 需要 2700 步 (= 1000 × 2.71) 匹配 KL 1000 步的总 FLOPs。这是 plan_003 Reviewer Concern #2 defense 的基础。\\n\\n## Checkpoints\\n- KL-only predictor (s42): `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\\n- SPON biases (seq128): `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/spon_kl_s42/spon_biases.pt` (928KB)\\n- SPON biases (seq2048): `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/spon_2048_s42/` (pending path confirm)\\n- BCE+comp predictor: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/bce_comp_staged_s42/predictor_bce_comp.pt`\\n- SPON eval script: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/eval_spon.py`\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3650, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distill_sparse_swiglu\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 2026 cycle)\\n- Deadline: 2026-05-25 (ARR submission)\\n- Track: Efficient Methods for NLP\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n1. **KL-trained sparsity predictors eliminate the need for error compensation networks**: At 50% model-level sparsity on SwiGLU LLMs, a KL-trained contextual predictor (no compensation) matches or exceeds BCE+compensation quality (FastForward-style), eliminating 67.2M compensation parameters (~256MB, 32 layers × bottleneck MLP) and per-layer GEMV overhead at inference. All quality comparisons must be presented alongside inference cost (params, FLOPs, latency) to prevent \\\"unfair comparison\\\" attacks. (Headline claim)\\n\\n2. **KL predictor subsumes most of SPON's static correction signal**: SPON's input-independent bias correction provides only modest additional recovery (7.3%, absolute 0.23 PPL) on top of a KL-trained contextual predictor at 50% sparsity. This indicates that output-distribution distillation already captures the average-case drift that static biases target. Strengthens Claim 1: KL's coupled gradient structure learns both per-input variation AND average-case patterns, making separate static correction largely redundant. (Evidence: spon_2048_s42, D010)\\n\\n3. **KL advantage grows with sparsity level (testable prediction)**: BCE's per-neuron factorized gradient (mean-field analog) misses joint neuron interactions scaling as O(s^2 D^2). We predict KL-vs-BCE delta follows approximately quadratic curve in sparsity level s — to be validated by 7-level sparsity sweep (plan_008). If empirical scaling deviates from quadratic (linear, log, or phase transition), the actual pattern is itself a finding; abstract/intro will report observed scaling, not assumed quadratic.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: SwiGLU activation sparsity enables 40-60% neuron pruning at inference, but training-free heuristics (WINA/TEAL) leave a 6+ point quality gap, while learned BCE predictors require auxiliary compensation networks adding ~256MB overhead (FastForward).\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: (a) Training-free (WINA, TEAL, R-Sparse): no training cost, but quality gap too large for deployment. (b) Static learned (SPON): zero-overhead bias correction via KL distillation, but input-independent — cannot adapt to per-input neuron importance. (c) Contextual learned (FastForward): BCE predictor + compensation MLP, near-dense quality but inference overhead from compensation network. Gap: no prior work trains the contextual predictor itself with KL, and no work quantifies how much static correction remains useful once a coupled-loss contextual predictor is in place.\\n\\n- **Our framing / headline question**: \\\"Does output-distribution distillation (KL) make error compensation unnecessary for contextual sparsity prediction?\\\" The theoretical grounding: BCE gradient dL_BCE/ds_j depends only on neuron j (factorized), while KL gradient dL_KL/ds_j couples all active neurons through d_logits/dh * dh/dm — a mathematical property of the gradient structure, not an analogy. This means KL captures joint interaction effects that BCE misses, especially at high sparsity.\\n\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"On [benchmarks], a KL-trained predictor at 50% model-level sparsity achieves [X] quality (within [Y]% of dense) without compensation, while BCE+compensation achieves [Z] quality but adds 67.2M parameters ([W] ms/token overhead) — demonstrating that output-distribution distillation subsumes error compensation, enabling a simpler, lighter inference pipeline with strictly better efficiency-quality tradeoff.\\\"\\n\\n## Two-Dimensional Taxonomy (Conceptual Contribution)\\n\\n|                          | Decomposed (BCE) | Intermediate (MSE) | Coupled (KL) |\\n|--------------------------|:-----------------:|:-------------------:|:-------------:|\\n| Static                   | —                 | —                   | SPON          |\\n| Per-layer                | —                 | ShadowLLM           | —             |\\n| Per-token contextual     | FastForward       | —                   | **Ours**      |\\n\\nPositions prior work and ours on two axes: correction granularity (static -> per-token) x training signal coupling (decomposed/BCE -> coupled/KL). Highlights the empty cell we fill.\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n1. **\\\"Incremental / no new algorithm\\\"**: Mitigate with formal decomposition framework, quantitative s^2 prediction, and comprehensive four-quadrant outcome analysis showing all results are informative.\\n\\n2. **\\\"KL wins only because more compute\\\"**: Addressed by iso-compute ablation (plan_003) giving BCE equivalent total FLOPs via extended training.\\n\\n3. **\\\"Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation, not KL, explains gains\\\"**: Addressed by STE baseline (plan_002) isolating loss function contribution.\\n\\n4. **\\\"Single model / limited generalization\\\"**: Addressed by Mistral-7B-v0.3 validation (plan_009) with different architecture (GQA + sliding window).\\n\\n5. **\\\"Effect size may not hold on downstream tasks\\\"**: Full benchmark suite (PPL + 5 discriminative + LongBench + MT-Bench) covers all axes.\\n\\n6. **\\\"Unfair comparison: BCE+comp close to KL but at higher inference cost\\\"**: All result tables and figures must co-report quality AND inference cost (compensation params, per-token FLOPs, end-to-end latency). Efficiency-accuracy Pareto frontier (plan_007) is the definitive visualization. Even if BCE+comp matches KL on PPL, KL wins on the Pareto frontier — that IS the claim.\\n\\n## Venue Fit\\n- EMNLP Efficient Methods track: strong fit for activation sparsity + distillation\\n- NLP-specific evaluation (LongBench long-context, MT-Bench multi-turn) strengthens EMNLP fit vs pure systems venue\\n- Novelty ceiling acknowledged: contribution is systematic composition + rigorous ablation, not new architecture\\n\", \"total_chars\": 5810, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```
